75 // The scalar overloads of the <cmath> family are the std ones, brought in by using-declarations rather than
76 // nda function templates: libc++ declares generic templates like pow(A1, A2) in the global namespace, which would
77 // tie with such templates for unqualified calls under `using namespace nda`. The using-declarations must follow the
78 // <cmath>, <complex> and <cstdlib> includes, since they only capture overloads declared before them.
79 #ifdef NDA_DOXYGEN
80 /**
81 * @brief Function pow for nda::Scalar types (uses `std::pow`).
82 */
83 template <Scalar S, Scalar P>
84 auto pow(S s, P p);
85 #endif
86 using std::pow;
87
